# W3 exit: every key moved by more than 5% against the step-0 base

Generated by `legion_check.py exit-table` (docs/legion-exit-tables.md, "W3 exit"): base = the W3 step-0 sim
with the exit observer (branch `w3b4-base`), new = the task-w3-b2 final head, all 91 scenarios, both modes, the
gate offsets (small-count keys on their median, the rest on the core five). *intended*: the A2/B outcome and
work families of user decisions 2 and 3 (arrival, completion, settle, convoy and the classes W3 adds or deletes);
every other move is *incidental* -- each loss among them carries its `accepted_regressions` reason in
`tools/scenarios/baseline.json`. No Retail key moved.
